Attestation must echo the current index_sha256, entry_sequence_sha256, and entry_count.

===/<repository>/include/umminfer/audio/===
audio.hpp[LG6T]: F:Declare WAV/PCM16 conversion helpers that wrap, validate, and strip RIFF/WAVE containers for the ASR audio path. | R:code:src/audio/audio.cpp, code:src/client/asr.cpp, code:src/serve/realtime_api.cpp, code:src/serve/openai_api.cpp, code:include/umminfer/client/client.hpp, code:tests/unified_contract_test.cpp | A:WavPcm16View; wav_from_pcm16; wav_from_pcm16(pcm, sample_rate); parse_wav_pcm16; pcm16_from_wav | S:Throw std::invalid_argument on invalid input; parse_wav_pcm16 aliases the caller's buffer without copying, so that buffer must outlive the view. The rate overload accepts 1..2147483647 Hz.

release.md[SV8S]: F:Specifies build hosts, artifact layout, publish script and verification steps for publishing ummInfer release artifacts. | R:code:tools/publish-release.sh, code:build.zig, code:native/stage_cli/CMakeLists.txt | A:tools/publish-release.sh; dist/<version>/; SHA256SUMS; MANIFEST.md; FORCE=1; gh release create | S:A rebuild of the same commit gives the same file set but not the same bytes: the macOS linker writes a new Mach-O UUID and nvcc adds per-compilation anonymous-namespace suffixes. SHA256SUMS pins the published bytes; do not use a rebuild hash as an equality test. The script refuses a dirty tree. Tags are immutable; publish a new patch instead of force-pushing a cloned tag. The release repository holds artifacts, docs and checksums only, no source or weights.

===/<repository>/include/umminfer/relay/===

Unknown relay types raise ProtocolError.

wire.cpp[MN8M]: F:Encodes and decodes MessagePack Value objects and provides schema field validation helpers | R:code:include/umminfer/proto/wire.hpp, code:src/util/codec.cpp, code:src/obs/metrics.cpp, code:src/client/http.cpp, code:include/umminfer/proto/messages.hpp | A:Value::encode; Value::decode; pack; unpack; require_field; require_non_negative_int | S:unpack rejects trailing bytes and a non-str map key; a Tensor is decoded only from a map with exactly the five encoder fields and a true __tensor__ mark, so any other map keeps every key; a uint64 above INT64_MAX throws instead of wrapping.

===/<repository>/src/relay/===
relay.cpp[MP8L]: F:Implements the POSIX shared-memory and CUDA IPC relays, the credit allocator and the create_relay backend factory | R:code:include/umminfer/relay/relay.hpp, code:src/comm/comm.cpp, code:src/pipeline/pipeline.cpp, code:tests/cuda_bench.cpp, code:docs/cuda-ipc.md | A:ShmRelay::put; ShmRelay::get; CudaIpcRelay::put; CudaIpcRelay::get and close; CreditAllocator::acquire, release and close; create_relay | S:Both relays carry host-serialized bytes only; cuda_ipc rejects a non-cpu source or destination and create_relay throws without UMMINFER_WITH_CUDA. A POSIX shm name is hashed to stay under the 31-byte macOS PSHMNAMLEN cap, and a block is never 0 bytes. An abandoned, timed-out or unacked cuda slot leaves the pool and is never freed or reused, and the relay becomes failed so later put throws until the sender restarts. acquire throws after close so a waiter unblocks. ShmRelay ignores slot_size_mb and allocates per transfer.
